# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-06T07:18:42+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 21:54:00 -0300 — Mulheres defendem articulação em rede para ampliar participação
  Na conferência internacional “Mulheres que movem a justiça”, realizada até sexta-feira (6) em Brasília, comunicadoras defenderam a articulação de redes de apoio entre mulheres como estratégia para combater o machismo e ampliar a participação feminina nos espaços de poder. O evento promove debates sobre uma justiça mais igualitária no Brasil. Segundo as participantes, os profissionais que trabalham com produção de informação também devem atuar no combate a mensagens misóginas e à desinformação. A presidente da Empresa Brasil de Comunicação (EBC), Antonia Pellegrino, contextualizou hoje (5), em mesa de debates, que a união feminina foi capaz de grandes feitos históricos, como as conquistas garantidas pelas parlamentares que participaram do que ficou conhecido como “lobby do batom”. - Thu, 05 Nov 2026 21:30:00 -0300 — Equipe da Agência Brasil vence Seleção Petrobras de Jornalismo
  O repórter Rafael Cardoso e a fotógrafa Tânia Rêgo estão entre os vencedores da Seleção Petrobras de Jornalismo. Eles receberão uma bolsa para desenvolver reportagem com o tema Ciência e Energias do Futuro. O resultado da seleção foi divulgado nesta quinta-feira (5). Ao todo, 14 propostas de reportagens foram contempladas em 12 estados do país. Os jornalistas ou equipes de jornalistas selecionados receberão bolsas no valor de R$ 20 mil reais cada para a produção das reportagens. A bolsa permitirá que a equipe desenvolva conteúdos especiais. A proposta selecionada é Quem mantém a luz acesa? Ciência, energia solar e autonomia Baniwa no Rio Negro.   https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/geral/noticia/2026-11/equipe-da-agencia-brasil-vence-selecao-petrobras-de-jornalismo
- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 20:59:00 -0300 — Mendonça concede prisão domiciliar a investigado por descontos no INSS
  O ministro André Mendonça, do Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF), decidiu nesta quinta-feira (5) conceder prisão domiciliar ao empresário Maurício Camisotti, um dos investigados pelos descontos irregulares nos benefícios do Instituto Nacional do Seguro Social (INSS). Na decisão, que ainda está em sigilo, o ministro atendeu ao pedido da defesa para substituir a prisão preventiva por medidas cautelares, como uso de tornozeleira eletrônica e proibição de contato com outros investigados. Camisotti está preso desde setembro de 2025 . Notícias relacionadas: Fachin devolve relatoria do caso do INSS a Mendonça e mantém Master. - Thu, 05 Nov 2026 19:47:00 -0300 — Skatista Duda Ribeiro é prata na Olimpíada da Juventude, em Dacar
  A quinta-feira (5) foi prateada para a skatista brasileira Duda Ribeiro na Olimpíada da Juventude, em Dacar (Senegal). A atleta foi vice-campeã no skate street ao somar 138.28 pontos na final. Completaram o pódio a chinesa Zhu Yuanling (149.82 pontos) com o ouro e a canadense Evie Pritchard com o bronze (137.79). A prata conquistada pela skatista é a terceira medalha do Brasil na competição – Clarisse Vallim foi ouro no judô e Ana Júlia Amaral bronze na natação. “Estou muito feliz e muito grata por ter acertado as minhas manobras e ter conseguido dar o meu melhor”, disse Duda, que no mês passado conquistou o ouro nos Jogos Sul-Americanos em Santa Fé (Argentina).   Introducing the Planet AI App: Search. Explore. Act. HOME News Introducing the Planet AI App: Search. Explore. Act. Karla King November 5, 2026 • 5 min read See what you can discover. Sign up and start exploring at ai.planet.com. News News AI chat apps are now incredibly adept at answering our questions about human knowledge. But for all their power, they are fundamentally blind to the physical world. If a farmer asks how to improve their crop yield, a chatbot will give the agricultural theory but know nothing about their field today. Same for a first responder facing a flood or a journalist investigating a landslide. In short, today’s chatbots live only in the digital world. That’s why I’m excited to share that my team at Planet has built an application that takes the power of the AI chat app into the physical domain. Back A Robotic Arm That Can Walk Across Spacecraft? Rocket Lab Built It Share this Future space missions could soon get a big leg up — or arm, rather — thanks to robotics developed by Rocket Lab in collaboration with NASA. Unlike traditional robotic arms that remain fixed in place, this version is designed to move autonomously across spacecraft and operate in zero or partial gravity, performing critical tasks like repairs, upgrades, and assembly. Rocket Lab Robotics (formerly Motiv Space Systems) developed the arm with versatility in mind. It can manipulate objects, use tools, and even perform complex tasks like repairing or upgrading space systems. Funded through NASA’s Small Business Innovation Re
  https://www.rocketlabusa.com/updates/a-robotic-arm-that-can-walk-across-spacecraft-rocket-lab-built-it/

The others are testing heat protection materials and navigation systems for when the capsules plunge back through Earth’s atmosphere at hypersonic speeds. #### Universe Today (space_press, en, feed)

- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 19:26:56 +0000 — Asteroids' Oddball Shapes Make Deflecting Them Challenging
  In a pinch, we could use a stand-off nuclear blast to redirect a dangerous asteroid on short notice. There's no atmospheric shock wave in space, but the x-ray pulse could still deflect the space rock. But asteroids aren't round and uniform, so their weird shapes make it difficult.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/asteroids-oddball-shapes-make-deflecting-them-challenging
- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 17:11:33 +0000 — A Mighty Wind Blew Away Gas and Made Early Galaxies Brighter
  When astronomers looked at photos of the early universe taken by the James Webb Space Telescope, they found something odd: an overabundance of bright galaxies about 400 to 500 million years after the Big Bang. But these galaxies should have been far less common so soon after the Big Bang. Researchers may have figured this out: Powerful ultraviolet (Lyman-alpha) radiation produced by dense clusters of young stars acted like a leaf blower, driving light-blocking gas and dust out of the galaxy and thereby making the galaxies appear brighter.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/a-mighty-wind-blew-away-gas-and-made-early-galaxies-brighter
- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 15:08:44 +0000 — Interstellar Visitor 3I/ATLAS Shows Water Ice Behaves the Same Across Alien Star Systems
  Targets of opportunity sometimes happen in astronomy, and we got a major one when the third interstellar object, 3I/ATLAS, was discovered in July 2025. And it just so happens that in the intervening years between the last known interstellar visitor (2I/Borisov in 2019), we got a major upgrade in our observational capabilities with the launch of the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST). A new paper from a team of researchers led by Dr. Silvia Protopapa of NASA’s Goddard Space Flight Center and the Southwest Research Institute, and published in The Astrophysical Journal Letters, details JWST’s findings on the water ice content of this most recent visitor from beyond our solar system.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/interstellar-visitor-3iatlas-shows-water-ice-behaves-the-same-across-alien-star-systems
- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 12:42:40 +0000 — Exoplanets Can't Hide From The JWST's 'Digital Twin'
  Finding dim exoplanets hidden in the glare of their stars is an ongoing challenge in astronomy. Telescopes use coronagraphs to block out starlight, but even they have their limitations. Researchers have developed a new technique with the JWST that can find dim exoplanets more easily than ever.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/exoplanets-cant-hide-from-the-jwsts-digital-twin

  SETI can be more effective the more the targets in the search are refined. SETI's newest survey effort uses game theory to inform its search. It's based on the idea that other ETIs would almost certainly use the transit method to detect habitable worlds like Earth.

  For a long time, planetary scientists thought that giant impacts drove a young planet's atmosphere away into space with all the heat they generated. But it turns out the opposite could be true. The mass a young planet gains through a giant impact increases its gravitational hold on its atmosphere.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/giant-impacts-help-rocky-planets-retain-their-atmospheres
- Wed, 04 Nov 2026 13:22:25 +0000 — A Virtual Lifeless Earth Will Help Scientists Find Life on Exoplanets
  Researchers have created a model lifeless Earth that couples the atmosphere with interior planetary processes. The model can reproduce Earth's modern habitable state without including life, showing that a planet that appears habitable isn't necessarily inhabited. The tool will be useful when and if the Habitable Worlds Observatory is launched, by helping identify false positives.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/a-virtual-lifeless-earth-will-help-scientists-find-life-on-exoplanets

  The Vera Rubin Observatory has detected two more of the Milky Ways' dim, hard to see satellites. One is an ultra-faint dwarf galaxy, and one is a globular cluster. Una de las empresas intervenidas es itti. El procedimiento fue acompañado por el comisario Diosnel Alarcón, jefe del Departamento de Cibercrimen de la Policía Nacional, y estuvo encabezado por los fiscales Manuel Doldán, Jorge Arce y Francisco Cabrera. Los intervinientes encontraron desmanteladas las oficinas de itti, s in embargo lograron incautar algunos equipos informáticos que permanecían en el lugar. Igualmente la Fiscalía volvió a allanar la casa de Miguel Vázquez. ### BRA

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 21:54:00 -0300 — Mulheres defendem articulação em rede para ampliar participação
  Na conferência internacional “Mulheres que movem a justiça”, realizada até sexta-feira (6) em Brasília, comunicadoras defenderam a articulação de redes de apoio entre mulheres como estratégia para combater o machismo e ampliar a participação feminina nos espaços de poder. O evento promove debates sobre uma justiça mais igualitária no Brasil. Segundo as participantes, os profissionais que trabalham com produção de informação também devem atuar no combate a mensagens misóginas e à desinformação. A presidente da Empresa Brasil de Comunicação (EBC), Antonia Pellegrino, contextualizou hoje (5), em mesa de debates, que a união feminina foi capaz de grandes feitos históricos, como as conquistas garantidas pelas parlamentares que participaram do que ficou conhecido como “lobby do batom”. - Thu, 05 Nov 2026 21:30:00 -0300 — Equipe da Agência Brasil vence Seleção Petrobras de Jornalismo
  O repórter Rafael Cardoso e a fotógrafa Tânia Rêgo estão entre os vencedores da Seleção Petrobras de Jornalismo. Eles receberão uma bolsa para desenvolver reportagem com o tema Ciência e Energias do Futuro. O resultado da seleção foi divulgado nesta quinta-feira (5). Ao todo, 14 propostas de reportagens foram contempladas em 12 estados do país. Os jornalistas ou equipes de jornalistas selecionados receberão bolsas no valor de R$ 20 mil reais cada para a produção das reportagens. A bolsa permitirá que a equipe desenvolva conteúdos especiais. A proposta selecionada é Quem mantém a luz acesa? Ciência, energia solar e autonomia Baniwa no Rio Negro.   https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/geral/noticia/2026-11/equipe-da-agencia-brasil-vence-selecao-petrobras-de-jornalismo
- Thu, 05 Nov 2026 20:59:00 -0300 — Mendonça concede prisão domiciliar a investigado por descontos no INSS
  O ministro André Mendonça, do Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF), decidiu nesta quinta-feira (5) conceder prisão domiciliar ao empresário Maurício Camisotti, um dos investigados pelos descontos irregulares nos benefícios do Instituto Nacional do Seguro Social (INSS). Na decisão, que ainda está em sigilo, o ministro atendeu ao pedido da defesa para substituir a prisão preventiva por medidas cautelares, como uso de tornozeleira eletrônica e proibição de contato com outros investigados. - Thu, 05 Nov 2026 19:47:00 -0300 — Skatista Duda Ribeiro é prata na Olimpíada da Juventude, em Dacar
  A quinta-feira (5) foi prateada para a skatista brasileira Duda Ribeiro na Olimpíada da Juventude, em Dacar (Senegal). A atleta foi vice-campeã no skate street ao somar 138.28 pontos na final. Completaram o pódio a chinesa Zhu Yuanling (149.82 pontos) com o ouro e a canadense Evie Pritchard com o bronze (137.79). A prata conquistada pela skatista é a terceira medalha do Brasil na competição – Clarisse Vallim foi ouro no judô e Ana Júlia Amaral bronze na natação. “Estou muito feliz e muito grata por ter acertado as minhas manobras e ter conseguido dar o meu melhor”, disse Duda, que no mês passado conquistou o ouro nos Jogos Sul-Americanos em Santa Fé (Argentina). - undated — NTS suspende investimentos no país e ajuíza medida contra a ANP
  NTS suspende investimentos no país e ajuíza medida contra a ANP - Agência iNFRA Ir para o conteúdo 05/11/2026 | 19h47 NTS suspende investimentos no país e ajuíza medida contra a ANP Foto: NTS/Divulgação Marisa Wanzeller e Ana Luísa Egues, da Agência iNFRA A NTS comunicou ao mercado nesta quinta-feira (5) que irá suspender investimentos da ordem de R$ 12 bilhões em ampliação da malha de gasodutos em decorrência de decisão da ANP (Agência Nacional do Petróleo, Gás Natural e Biocombustíveis) que reduz a remuneração das transportadoras de gás. A empresa também informou que irá ajuizar medida judicial contra a reguladora. A ANP definiu no último mês a metodologia para definição da BRA (Bases Regulatórias de Ativos) das transportadoras de gás natural, com base na metodologia RCM (Método de Capital Recuperado, na sigla em inglês). O método escolhido desfavorece as empresas, por reduzir o reajus

  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. - undated — European Bodyguard Sats are Closer Thank You Think
  European Bodyguard Sats are Closer Thank You Think Skip to content Last week, we covered Europe’s newfound efforts to fund greater defensive—and offensive —capabilities in orbit. This week, we’re starting to get a sense of when those capabilities might be ready to fly. Spoiler alert: It’s sooner than you probably think. On Wednesday, Swiss propulsion startup PAVE Space announced its plans to launch a demo mission of its IBEX spacecraft—designed as a highly maneuverable platform to host commercial and defense payloads—as early as February 2028.&nbsp; The mission will pave the way (pun intended) for future missions to conduct on-orbit “reconnaissance, co-orbital denial—acting like a bodyguard—jamming, and capture or neutralization of a hostile spacecraft,” according to the announcement. Rapid fire: For a company that was founded in 2024, PAVE’s launch timeline is an aggressive signal that
  https://payloadspace.com/european-bodyguard-sats-are-closer-thank-you-think/
- undated — CesiumAstro Raises $730M, Reaches Valuation Above $4.5B
  CesiumAstro Raises $730M, Reaches Valuation Above $4.5B Skip to content MERRITT ISLAND, FL—CesiumAstro plans to build and deploy a new constellation—called Synchronicity—consisting of 737 satellites. The company made a downpayment on it Thursday, closing a $730M financing round. Janus Henderson led the equity round, which included additional participation from Insight Partners, Sands Capital, Sumitomo Corporation of Americas, Woven Capital, Incharge Capital, Magnetar, AV, and NewSpace Capital. The round included a $100M debt facility provided by the Export-Import Bank of the United States and JPMorgan in addition to $630M in equity financing. Small step, giant leap: The announcement comes just over a month after CesiumAstro sent its first satellite to orbit. Element-1 launched on SpaceX’s Transporter-18 rideshare in October, on a mission designed to pave the way for the full Synchronicit
  https://payloadspace.com/cesiumastro-raises-730m-reaches-valuation-above-4-5b/
- undated — Exclusive: Orbital Taps Orca Aerospace for Onboard Autonomy Software
  Exclusive: Orbital Taps Orca Aerospace for Onboard Autonomy Software Skip to content Orbital’s data center sats are getting a killer navigator. Satellite autonomy startup Orca Aerospace announced a deal worth up to $20M with LA-based Orbital to put its software on Orbital’s planned data-center fleet .&nbsp; Orca’s says its software will act as an autonomous navigator, linking each sat’s flight software to Orbital’s ground control. It will enable satellites to handle maneuvers, tasks, and fault responses on their own, consulting humans only when needed. “Onboard autonomy helps us make it so that collision avoidance is an easy thing that doesn’t require a large team of operators,” Orca Aerospace CEO and cofounder Jacob Ososke told Payload. “For Orbital to scale at the size that it needs to, and for it to scale safely, Orca’s technology stack is something that really needs to live onboard.”
  https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-orbital-taps-orca-aerospace-for-onboard-autonomy-software/

- undated — Former NASA Chief Economist is New Head of Canadian Space Agency
  Former NASA Chief Economist is New Head of Canadian Space Agency &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com Alexander MacDonald is the new President of the Canadian Space Agency. Canadian-born MacDonald was NASA&#8217;s Chief Economist until December 2024. Alex MacDonald (from LinkedIn ). The Canadian Government announced MacDonald&#8217;s selection this evening. He succeeds Lisa Campbell, who had held the position since 2020. Her five-year term was extended by one year. A dual Canadian-U.S. citizen born in Canada, MacDonald was NASA&#8217;s first Chief Economist after serving in several other positions at the agency. As Chief Economist, he was part of NASA&#8217;s Office of Technology, Policy, and Strategy, which was eliminated by DOGE last year although MacDonald left the agency a few months earlier. - undated — November 5, 2026 | Second spacewalk for CSA astronaut Joshua Kutryk
  Second spacewalk for CSA astronaut Joshua Kutryk | Canadian Space Agency --> Top of page Second spacewalk for CSA astronaut Joshua Kutryk Credit: NASA / CSA 2026-11-05 Earlier today, Canadian Space Agency ( CSA ) astronaut Colonel Joshua Kutryk conducted the second spacewalk of his career alongside NASA astronaut Anil Menon, just seven days after their previous extravehicular activity on October&nbsp;29 . With today's six&nbsp;hours and 59&nbsp;minutes spacewalk that led to the installation of another solar array, the upgrade campaign launched in 2021 to enhance the Station's power generation capacity is now complete. This technology, first tested less than a decade ago, is now helping power more scientific research aboard the International Space Station and extending its operational capabilities, while also informing solar power systems that could support future missions. Joshua and Ani

- undated — Minister McGuinty Announces Strategic Investments in Sovereign Space Launch
  Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. McGuinty, Minister of National Defence, announced an historic $200 million investment in core infrastructure for a Canadian-owned spaceport, along with other major new space-related capabilities and initiatives. This marks a significant step forward in Canada’s sovereign space program as outlined in [_Security, Sovereignty, Prosperity: Canada’s Defence Industrial Strategy_](https://www.canada.ca/en/department-national-defence/corporate/reports-publications/industrial-strategy.html). ### Canadian-owned spaceport Demand for timely and resilient launch options are at historic highs worldwide and sovereign access to space has become a critical capability underpinning nati
  https://www.canada.ca/en/department-national-defence/news/2026/03/minister-mcguinty-announces-strategic-investments-in-sovereign-space-launch.html

Credit: MDA Space MDA Space raised its 2026 revenue forecast on Thursday after a better-than-expected third quarter that brought in $498 million, up 22 per cent from a year earlier. MDA Space now expects revenue of $1.90 billion to $1.95 billion this year, up from $1.8 billion to $1.9 billion in August, or growth of roughly 18 per cent. The gain came from home: sales to Canadian customers rose 44 per cent, with Telesat&#8217;s Lightspeed constellation the main driver, while sales to U.S. customers fell 19 per cent. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . - undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php
- undated — Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS : Der Erdbeobachtungssatellit PEGASUS feierte Juni 2021 bereits seinen 4. Geburtstag.
  Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS - Austria in Space Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Der Erdbeobachtungssatellit PEGASUS feierte Juni 2021 bereits seinen 4. Geburtstag. Pegasus ist jetzt bereits vier Jahre aktiv. (Perihelion ) PEGASUS ist Teil des Satellitenprojekts QB50 und wurde 2017 von Indien aus in die Umlaufbahn gebracht. Eines der Hauptziele des Projekts QB50 war es, Studierenden auf der ganzen Welt die Möglichkeit zu geben, einen Satelliten zu entwickeln und zu betreiben. Das Ergebnis dieses Projekts in Österreich war PEGASUS, der von Studierenden der Fachhochschule Wiener Neustadt, der Technischen Universität Wien und der Universität Wien in Zusammenarbeit mit dem Österreichischen Weltraumforum entwickelt wurde. Der Satellit ist noch immer im Einsatz und überträgt weiterhin Daten. Projekt: QB50, ein Netzwerk von 36 Nanosatellit
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/happybirthdaypegasus.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op

  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha

  Application for Diplomatic Clearance (DIC) for Foreign State Aircraft - Directorate of Defence - The Luxembourg Government Share Share on Facebook Share on Instagram Share on X Share on BlueSky Share on LinkedIn Share by email Copy the link Last update 25.09.2025 Who Needs a Diplomatic Clearance? Any foreign state aircraft intending to enter Luxembourg airspace for overflight or landing. This includes: Military, customs, or police aircraft State-owned or state-used aircraft for public missions Issuing Authority: Overflight DIC: Directorate of Defence of the Ministry of Foreign and European Affairs, Defence, Development Cooperation and Foreign Trade Landing DIC: Directorate of Defence in consultation with Directorate of Civil Aviation (DAC) Application Procedure: Diplomatic missions must submit a verbal note including the "EUROPEAN UNION DIPLOMATIC CLEARANCE (DIC)" form to the Directorate

- undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. - undated — Wyścig chińskich rakiet. Dwa kolejne systemy blisko debiutu
  Wyścig chińskich rakiet. Dwa kolejne systemy blisko debiutu Menu Minione lato było świadkiem pełnego wejścia Chińskiej Republiki Ludowej do rywalizacji z USA w obszarze rakiet wielokrotnego użytku, dotychczas zdominowanym przez amerykańskie SpaceX. W lipcu br. pierwszy stopień państwowej rakiety Chang Zheng 10B został odzyskany na platformie morskiej. Wyczyn ten zaledwie miesiąc później powtórzyła komercyjna Zhuque-3 firmy LandSpace, której pierwszy stopień wylądował na lądzie. Sukces Chin. Odzyskano pierwszy stopień rakiety Zhuque-3 W najbliższych miesiącach gama chińskich rakiet wielokrotnego użytku może przewyższyć amerykańską pod względem ich liczby dzięki szybkim postępom w rozwoju przynajmniej dwóch kolejnych komercyjnych platform nośnych. 26 października firma iSpace ogłosiła zakończenie testów obciążeniowych kluczowych elementów konstrukcyjnych rakiety Hyperbola-3 , po wrześniowy

- undated — Rosyjskie manewry na orbicie. „Podobnych zdarzeń będzie więcej” [WYWIAD]
  Rosyjskie manewry na orbicie. „Podobnych zdarzeń będzie więcej” [WYWIAD] Menu W drugiej części wywiadu z płk Leszkiem Paszkowskim, szefem Agencji Geoprzestrzennego Rozpoznania i Usług Satelitarnych (ARGUS) , poruszamy m.in. temat rosyjskich manewrów na orbicie, możliwości reagowania na tego typu zagrożenia oraz ochrony polskiej infrastruktury kosmicznej. Pierwszą część rozmowy, poświęconą rozwojowi polskich zdolności satelitarnych, planom organizacyjnym ARGUS oraz współpracy ze Stanami Zjednoczonymi, można przeczytać tutaj. Wojsko Polskie buduje własne zdolności kosmiczne. „To dopiero początek” [WYWIAD] Mateusz Mitkow, redaktor naczelny Space24.pl: W ostatnim czasie mogliśmy obserwować manewry rosyjskich satelitów w pobliżu jednostki ICEYE-X36, wykorzystywanej m.in. do obrazowania radarowego na potrzeby Ukrainy. - undated — X-70 Fractal. NASA ujawnia nowy samolot eksperymentalny
  X-70 Fractal. NASA ujawnia nowy samolot eksperymentalny Menu Nową konstrukcję zaprezentował Jared Isaacman, administrator NASA , podczas wydarzenia UP.Summit w Kennedy Space Center na Florydzie. X-70 Fractal ma poszerzyć możliwości amerykańskiej agencji kosmicznej w zakresie prowadzenia badań w locie i przyspieszyć weryfikację technologii, które mogą znaleźć zastosowanie w przyszłych generacjach samolotów. „X-70 Fractal zapewnia NASA załogową, wysoce wydajną platformę do testowania w rzeczywistych warunkach lotu zaawansowanych systemów sterowania, manewrowości oraz innych nowatorskich technologii […] Właśnie po to ludzie przychodzą do NASA: by tworzyć, uczyć się, a przede wszystkim – by latać ” – podkreślił Jared Isaacman. Starliner wróci do lotów z załogą. NASA wskazuje 2028 rok Jedną z najważniejszych cech X-70 ma być możliwość szybkiej rekonfiguracji systemów sterowania lotem . Pozwol

  Razpis Evropske vesoljske agencije namenjen slovenskim podjetjem in institucijam | GOV.SI Domov Novice Razpis Evropske vesoljske agencije namenjen slovenskim podjetjem in institucijam 2. 10. 2026 Ministrstvo za gospodarstvo, delo in šport Evropska vesoljska agencija (angleško European Space Agency - ESA) je objavila drugi razpis odkar je Slovenija polnopravna članica ESA za slovenska podjetja in institucije (angleško Requesting Party Activity - RPA) za zbiranje okvirnih predlogov projektov izbranih tipov aktivnosti z namenom krepitve vesoljskega sektorja v Sloveniji. RPA razpis je namenjen samo podjetjem in akademskim ter raziskovalnim organizacijam s sedežem v Sloveniji. Namen razpisa je krepitev slovenskega vesoljskega sektorja in spodbujanje vključitve obstoječih ter novih podjetij in organizacij, ki še niso aktivna na področju vesoljskih tehnologij.   Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. The new award funds a rapid flight experiment, which will fly ahead of the full Otter VLEO mission, Spence Wise, senior vice president of Space Missions for Redwire, told Via Satellite . It will be a short-duration flight, lasting less than 24 hours, targeted for late 2027. “This award funds the build and flight of a scaled spacecraft to test critical propulsion technologies sooner. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Sep 25, 2026 Destaques GGPEN e Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos assinam Carta de Intenção para promover colaboração em satélites, observação da Terra, capacitação e inovação. O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. O documento foi assinado pelo Director-Geral do GGPEN, Dr. Zolana Rui João, e pelo Director-Geral da Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos, Eng. - undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate

  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre.   NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection.   Grand Lomé : la police mobilisée pour empêcher le retour des occupants illégaux - République Togolaise Tendances: Diplomatie Lire la suite Zouréhatou Kassah-Traoré © republicoftogo.com Pas question de revivre l’échec de 2023. Face à la presse mercredi, la gouverneure du District autonome du Grand Lomé (DAGL), Zouréhatou Kassah-Traoré, a assuré que les espaces libérés le long des routes seraient, cette fois, durablement surveillés. L’opération de 2023, faute de suivi, s’était soldée par le retour rapide des occupants délogés. Désormais, les commissariats de police des 13 communes du Grand Lomé seront chargés de veiller, dès la fin de l’opération, à ce que les voies publiques restent dégagées. « Nous ne faisons pas cela pour faire plaisir à une quelconque autorité, mais pour notre propre capitale, pour qu’elle redevienne une ville accueillante, propre et sûre », a insisté la gouverneure. Z
  https://www.republicoftogo.com/toutes-les-rubriques/societe/grand-lome-la-police-mobilisee-pour-empecher-le-retour-des-occupants-illegaux

- undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html
- undated — Final Orbit manoeuvring of NVS-03 spacecraft
  Final Orbit manoeuvring of NVS-03 spacecraft Final Orbit manoeuvring of NVS-03 spacecraft Home / Final Orbit manoeuvring of NVS-03 spacecraft November 04, 2026 NVS-03 satellite was successfully injected into 170.3 km x 38080 km orbit by GSLV on October 30, 2026 at 23.51 hrs. NVS-03 is the third satellite in next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries indigenous atomic clock along with the procured clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to L5 and S band signals with coverage over Indian land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. Four orbit manoeuvres have been successfully completed with a cumulative LAM engine burn of 6164 seconds.   Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. #### TechNews (科技新報) (press, zh, newsroom)

- undated — SpaceX 豪擲 400 億美元布局 AI，攜手高盛、阿波羅打造科技基建融資新指標
  SpaceX 豪擲 400 億美元布局 AI，攜手高盛、阿波羅打造科技基建融資新指標 | TechNews 科技新報 據市場消息傳出，高盛集團（Goldman Sachs）與阿波羅全球管理（Apollo Global Management）正積極與投資人接洽，計劃出售太空探索技術公司（SpaceX）規模約 400 億美元 AI 晶片融資案的部分額度。該交易特別採用「多層次融資架構」，旨在將龐大債務排除於 SpaceX 的資產負債表之外（即表外融資），這也被業界視為有史以來規模最大的 GPU 融資安排之一。 根據相關報導指出，這筆巨額融資將專門用來支應 SpaceX 採購輝達（Nvidia）晶片。知情人士透露，在正式的銀行聯貸銷售程序啟動之前，兩家金融巨頭已開始探詢部分投資人的意願，評估其是否願意先行認購最高達 5 億美元的份額。 在具體的債務結構方面，該交易包含了一筆 83 億美元的優先擔保貸款，以及多個債券分層。其中，貸款部分由高盛負責主導，阿波羅則擔任主要的債券投資人，而知名資產管理巨頭 PIMCO（品浩）也參與了此融資行列。按照協議規畫，這批晶片預計將於 2027 年上半年交付，SpaceX 後續將以五年期的合約來承租這批設備。 在全球人工智慧投資熱潮持續發燒的當下，這筆天價融資案不僅備受市場矚目，更深刻凸顯出「私募資本」與「大型科技基礎建設」之間日益緊密的合作連結。 Goldman, Apollo in Talks to Sell SpaceX’s $40 Billion AI Deal （首圖來源： Unsplash ） 延伸閱讀： 馬斯克喊 SpaceX 籌得 400 億美元買輝達晶片！嗆新雲端將被淘汰 SpaceX 擬籌 400 億美元資金，目標購入大量輝達晶片以打造 AI 算力基建 算力需求大爆發，SpaceX 發動「突擊」加薪 20% 徵員工趕工擴建超級電腦 史上首見軌道 AI 資料中心，SpaceX 誓言 2027 讓輝達頂規算力升空 SpaceX 全面擁抱輝達晶片，布局太空 AI 算力 文章看完覺得有幫助，何不給我們一個鼓勵 請我們喝杯咖啡 想請我
  https://technews.tw/2026/11/05/spacex-ai-chip-financing/

  President Lee Jae Myung has acted on his words. He launched his own YouTube channel, President’s Y, with its first episode airing Thursday. Lee spent most of the 30-minute conversation with a female interviewer making the case for his social media-driven approach to politics and explaining how his direct engagement with the public could benefit the nation. He pushed back against criticism of his outreach efforts, stressing their necessity. “Like in physical space, there are people on social media,” he said. He also reaffirmed his belief that livestreaming Cabinet meetings has more benefits than drawbacks. - undated — Nepal-tagged vulture travels 600 km to India
  Nepal-tagged vulture travels 600 km to India Nepal-tagged vulture travels 600 km to India The Rising Nepal Fri, 6 November 2026 Share Change Font Sizes: The Rising Nepal Share: Fri, 6 November 2026 By A Staff Reporter, Kathmandu, Nov. 6: A satellite-tagged adult White-rumped Vulture, known as dangar giddha in Nepali, has travelled around 600 km from the western lowlands of Nepal to the Kangara forest in the Pong area of Himachal Pradesh, India. The vulture was recently recorded sitting on a nest in the forest. “This remarkable discovery provides valuable insights into the long-distance movement, habitat use, and breeding ecology of the species and highlights the importance of coordinated conservation efforts across international boundaries,” said Krishna Bhusal, Asia Regional Co-Chair of the IUCN SSC Vulture Specialist Group and Technical Advisory Panel member of SAVE. - undated — Ian Griffin explains the fate of Nasa's Swift satellite
  Ian Griffin explains the fate of Nasa&#39;s Swift satellite | RNZ Skip to main content science space 4 November 2026 Ian Griffin explains the fate of Nasa&#39;s Swift satellite From Morning Report , 7:54 am on 4 November 2026 At some point today a half-a-billion-dollar satellite is set to burn up somewhere over the Pacific Ocean after an attempt to save it failed. Nasa's Swift Observatory was launched in 2004 to study black holes and exploding stars. But ever since then its orbit has been slowly dropping, and in 2025 a last-ditch rescue to push it back up didn't work. Otago Museum director and astronomer Ian Griffin spoke to Ingrid Hipkiss.
